        print(header)
        print("  " + "─" * 68)
        for attr, label in steps:
            vals = [getattr(t, attr) for t in cell_timings]
            a = _agg(vals)
            marker = "  ◄◄" if "重点" in label else ""
            print(
                f"  {label:<40} {a['mean']:>7.1f}  {a['max']:>7.1f}  "
                f"{a['min']:>7.1f}  {a['p95']:>7.1f}  {a['total']:>9.1f}{marker}"
            )

        # Step 5a LLM 使用统计
        llm_cnt  = sum(1 for t in cell_timings if getattr(t, 'ctx_llm_used_llm', False))
        fallback_cnt = len(cell_timings) - llm_cnt
        print(f"\n  Step 5a LLM 调用统计: 实际调用={llm_cnt}  退回硬编码={fallback_cnt}")

        # constraint chain 方法分布
        if use_constraint_chain:
            from collections import Counter, defaultdict
            method_dist = Counter(t.method_used for t in cell_timings if t.method_used)
            unique_cnt  = sum(1 for t in cell_timings if t.is_unique)
            print(f"\n  约束方法分布 (共 {len(cell_timings)} cells, 唯一锁定 {unique_cnt}):")
            print(f"  {'方法名':<35} {'成功次数':>8} {'平均耗时ms':>12}")
            print("  " + "─" * 58)
            for method, cnt in method_dist.most_common():
                # 收集该方法的耗时字数据
                times = [
                    t.method_timings[method]
                    for t in cell_timings
                    if isinstance(t.method_timings, dict) and method in t.method_timings
                ]
                avg_ms = sum(times) / len(times) if times else 0.0
                is_unique_method = method not in ("count_fallback", "yesno_fallback",
                                                   "emergency_fallback")
                tag = " ✓唯一" if is_unique_method else " (fallback)"
                print(f"  {method:<35} {cnt:>8}     {avg_ms:>9.2f} ms{tag}")

            # 逆向统计：每个方法被尝试的次数（包括失败的）
            all_tried_counts: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
            for t in cell_timings:
                if isinstance(t.method_timings, dict):
                    for mname in t.method_timings:
                        all_tried_counts[mname] += 1
            print(f"\n  [各方法被计载(含未成功)次数和平均耗时]")
            print(f"  {'方法名':<35} {'被计载次':>8} {'平均耗时ms':>12}")
            print("  " + "─" * 58)
            for mname, tried in sorted(all_tried_counts.items(),
                                        key=lambda x: -x[1]):
                times = [
                    t.method_timings[mname]
                    for t in cell_timings
                    if isinstance(t.method_timings, dict) and mname in t.method_timings
                ]
                avg_ms = sum(times) / len(times) if times else 0.0
                success = method_dist.get(mname, 0)
                print(f"  {mname:<35} {tried:>8}     {avg_ms:>9.2f} ms  (success={success})")

    print(f"\n  生成 QA 对总数: {len(all_qa)}")
    print(f"{SEP}\n")


# =============================================================================
# 主流程
# =============================================================================

def run_pipeline(
    neo4j_uri: str,
    neo4j_user: str,
    neo4j_password: str,
    max_cells: int = 0,
    max_per_cell: int = 8,
    use_constraint_chain: bool = False,
    scene_name: str = "",
    frame_idx: int = 0,
    output_path: Optional[str] = None,
    target_coverage: float = 0.0,
    max_qa: int = 0,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    执行完整 gap pipeline 并返回计时 + QA 结果字典。

    Parameters
    ----------
    neo4j_uri / neo4j_user / neo4j_password : 连接参数
    max_cells      : 最多处理的 gap cell 数（0 = 不限）
    max_per_cell   : 每 cell 最多生成的模板 QA 对数
    use_constraint_chain : True 时启用 ConstraintChain
    scene_name, frame_idx : 写入 QA 元数据
    output_path    : JSON 输出路径（None = 不写出）
    target_coverage : 目标 edge 覆盖率 %（0 = 不限，如 30.0 表示达到 30% 即停）
    max_qa         : 目标 QA 对总数（0 = 不限，如 5000 表示生成 5000 条即停）
    """
    from neo4j import GraphDatabase  # type: ignore[import]
    from gap_pipeline.llm_client import LLMClient
    from gap_pipeline.scene_coverage import CoverageMap, SceneCoverageCalculator

    constraint_chain = None
    if use_constraint_chain:
        from gap_pipeline.constraint_methods import ConstraintChain
        constraint_chain = ConstraintChain()

    global_times: Dict[str, float] = {}

    # ── Step 0: 初始化连接 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    with _measure() as out:
        llm    = LLMClient()
        driver = GraphDatabase.driver(neo4j_uri, auth=(neo4j_user, neo4j_password))
    global_times["0_init_ms"] = out[0]
    logger.info("Step 0  init  %.1f ms", global_times["0_init_ms"])

    try:
        # ── Step 1: 场景枚举 Cypher（硬编码，不走 LLM）─────────────────────────
        with _measure() as out:
            from gap_pipeline.llm_client import LLMClient as _LC
            scene_cypher = _LC.build_scene_analysis_cypher()
        global_times["1_scene_cypher_ms"] = out[0]
        logger.info("Step 1  scene_cypher(hardcoded)  %.3f ms", global_times["1_scene_cypher_ms"])

        # ── Step 2: Neo4j 执行场景枚举 ────────────────────────────────────────
        with _measure() as out:
            with driver.session() as session:
                edge_records = [dict(rec) for rec in session.run(scene_cypher)]
        global_times["2_scene_neo4j_ms"] = out[0]
        logger.info(
            "Step 2  scene_neo4j  %.1f ms  edges=%d",
            global_times["2_scene_neo4j_ms"], len(edge_records),
        )

        # ── Step 3: CoverageMap 初始化 ────────────────────────────────────────
        with _measure() as out:
            cmap = CoverageMap()
            cmap.init_from_records(edge_records)
        global_times["3_cmap_init_ms"] = out[0]
        logger.info("Step 3  cmap_init  %.1f ms", global_times["3_cmap_init_ms"])

        # ── Step 4: 识别 gap cells ────────────────────────────────────────────
        with _measure() as out:
            gap_cells = cmap.get_gap_cells(level="edge")
        global_times["4_gap_detect_ms"] = out[0]
        logger.info(
            "Step 4  gap_detect  %.1f ms  gaps=%d",
            global_times["4_gap_detect_ms"], len(gap_cells),
        )

        if max_cells > 0:
            gap_cells = gap_cells[:max_cells]
            logger.info("⚙  max_cells=%d → 截取 %d cells", max_cells, len(gap_cells))

        # ── Step 5: per-cell 循环 ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        all_qa: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        cell_timings: List[_CellTiming] = []

        # 预算停止条件说明
        stop_reasons: List[str] = []
        if max_qa > 0:
            stop_reasons.append(f"max_qa={max_qa}")
        if target_coverage > 0:
            stop_reasons.append(f"target_coverage={target_coverage}%")
        if stop_reasons:
            logger.info("⚙  停止条件: %s", " | ".join(stop_reasons))

        total_edges = len(cmap._edge_counts)

        for i, cell in enumerate(gap_cells, 1):
            # ── 预算检查（每 cell 前） ──────────────────────────────────────────
            if max_qa > 0 and len(all_qa) >= max_qa:
                logger.info("  🛑 达到 max_qa=%d，已生成 %d 条，提前停止", max_qa, len(all_qa))
                break
            if target_coverage > 0:
                covered = sum(1 for c in cmap._edge_counts.values() if c > 0)
                current_rate = covered / total_edges * 100 if total_edges else 0
                if current_rate >= target_coverage:
                    logger.info(
                        "  🛑 达到目标覆盖率 %.1f%%（当前 %.2f%%），提前停止",
                        target_coverage, current_rate,
                    )
                    break

            src_id = cell.get("src_id", "?")
            tgt_id = cell.get("tgt_id", "?")
            logger.info("  cell %d/%d  %s→%s", i, len(gap_cells), src_id, tgt_id)
            try:
                qa_list, ct = _process_cell_timed(
                    cell=cell,
                    llm_client=llm,
                    driver=driver,
                    use_constraint_chain=use_constraint_chain,
                    constraint_chain=constraint_chain,
                    max_per_cell=max_per_cell,
                    scene_name=scene_name,
                    frame_idx=frame_idx,
                )
                all_qa.extend(qa_list)
                cell_timings.append(ct)
                # 实时更新 CoverageMap 以便覆盖率检查准确
                for qa in qa_list:
                    cmap.update(qa)
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                logger.warning("  cell %s→%s 失败: %s", src_id, tgt_id, exc)

        # ── Step 6: CoverageMap 最终统计（已在循环中实时更新）────────────────
        with _measure() as out:
            pass  # updates already done per-cell in the loop above
        global_times["6_cmap_update_ms"] = out[0]

        # ── 打印汇总 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        _print_summary(global_times, cell_timings, all_qa, use_constraint_chain)

        cov_stats = cmap.stats()
        logger.info("Coverage: %s", cov_stats)

        # ── 构建结果字典 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        # 逐方法计时汇总
        from collections import defaultdict
        _method_agg: Dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
        for ct in cell_timings:
            if isinstance(ct.method_timings, dict):
                for mname, ms in ct.method_timings.items():
                    _method_agg[mname].append(ms)
        per_method_timing = {
            mname: _agg(vals)
            for mname, vals in sorted(_method_agg.items())
        }

        # Step 5a LLM 使用统计
        llm_call_count = sum(1 for ct in cell_timings
                             if getattr(ct, 'ctx_llm_used_llm', False))

        result: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "global_times_ms":     global_times,
            "n_gap_cells":         len(gap_cells),
            "n_qa_generated":      len(all_qa),
            "coverage":            cov_stats,
            "step5a_llm_calls":    llm_call_count,
            "step5a_fallback_calls": len(cell_timings) - llm_call_count,
            "cell_timings":        [ct.to_dict() for ct in cell_timings],
            "per_cell_agg": {
                step: _agg([getattr(t, step) for t in cell_timings])
                for step in (
                    "ctx_llm_ms",
                    "ctx_neo4j_ms",
                    "cand_neo4j_ms",
                    "constraint_ms",
                    "template_fill_ms",
                    "total_ms",
                )
            },
            "per_method_timing":   per_method_timing,
            "qa_pairs": all_qa,
        }

        if output_path:
            _out = Path(output_path)
            _out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            _out.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            logger.info("结果已写入: %s", _out)

        return result

    finally:
        driver.close()


# =============================================================================
# CLI 入口
# =============================================================================

def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Gap Pipeline 主运行脚本（带全流程计时）",
    )
    p.add_argument("--neo4j-uri",      default="bolt://localhost:7800")
    p.add_argument("--neo4j-user",     default="neo4j")
    p.add_argument("--neo4j-password", default="87017563")
    p.add_argument(
        "--max-cells", type=int, default=0,
        help="最多处理的 gap cell 数（0 = 不限）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--max-per-cell", type=int, default=8,
        help="每 cell 最多生成的模板 QA 对数（默认 8）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--use-constraint-chain", action="store_true",
        help="启用 ConstraintChain（5c 候选查询 + 5d 约束收束），并单独计时",
    )
    p.add_argument("--scene-name", default="")
    p.add_argument("--frame-idx",  type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument(
        "--output", default=None,
        help="JSON 结果输出路径（含 QA 对 + 全部计时数据）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--target-coverage", type=float, default=0.0,
        help="目标 edge 覆盖率 %%（如 30.0 = 达到 30%% 后停止，0 = 不限）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--max-qa", type=int, default=0,
        help="目标 QA 对总数（如 5000 = 生成 5000 条后停止，0 = 不限）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--log-level",
        choices=["DEBUG", "INFO", "WARNING"],
        default="INFO",
    )
    return p.parse_args()


def main() -> None:
    args = _parse_args()
    logging.getLogger().setLevel(args.log_level)

    logger.info(
        "启动 Gap Pipeline  neo4j=%s  max_cells=%s  constraint_chain=%s",
        args.neo4j_uri, args.max_cells or "∞", args.use_constraint_chain,
    )

    run_pipeline(
        neo4j_uri=args.neo4j_uri,
        neo4j_user=args.neo4j_user,
        neo4j_password=args.neo4j_password,
        max_cells=args.max_cells,

# =============================================================================
# CLI 入口
# =============================================================================

def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Gap Pipeline 主运行脚本（带全流程计时）",
    )
    p.add_argument("--neo4j-uri",      default="bolt://localhost:7800")
    p.add_argument("--neo4j-user",     default="neo4j")
    p.add_argument("--neo4j-password", default="87017563")
    p.add_argument(
        "--max-cells", type=int, default=0,
        help="最多处理的 gap cell 数（0 = 不限）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--max-per-cell", type=int, default=8,
        help="每 cell 最多生成的模板 QA 对数（默认 8）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--use-constraint-chain", action="store_true",
        help="启用 ConstraintChain（5c 候选查询 + 5d 约束收束），并单独计时",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--use-cumulative-chain", action="store_true",
        help="启用 CumulativeConstraintChain（动态叠加约束，自动寻找最少属性组合）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--timing-log", default=None,
        help="计时日志路径（默认: output/timing_log.jsonl）",
    )
    p.add_argument("--scene-name", default="")
    p.add_argument("--frame-idx",  type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument(
        "--output", default=None,
        help="JSON 结果输出路径（含 QA 对 + 全部计时数据）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--target-coverage", type=float, default=0.0,
        help="目标 edge 覆盖率 %%（如 30.0 = 达到 30%% 后停止，0 = 不限）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--max-qa", type=int, default=0,
        help="目标 QA 对总数（如 5000 = 生成 5000 条后停止，0 = 不限）",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--log-level",
        choices=["DEBUG", "INFO", "WARNING"],
        default="INFO",
    )
    return p.parse_args()


def main() -> None:
    args = _parse_args()
    logging.getLogger().setLevel(args.log_level)

    chain_mode = (
        "cumulative" if args.use_cumulative_chain
        else ("fixed" if args.use_constraint_chain else "none")
    )
    logger.info(
        "启动 Gap Pipeline  neo4j=%s  max_cells=%s  chain_mode=%s",
        args.neo4j_uri, args.max_cells or "∞", chain_mode,
    )

    run_pipeline(
        neo4j_uri=args.neo4j_uri,
        neo4j_user=args.neo4j_user,
        neo4j_password=args.neo4j_password,
        max_cells=args.max_cells,
        max_per_cell=args.max_per_cell,
        use_constraint_chain=args.use_constraint_chain,
        scene_name=args.scene_name,
        frame_idx=args.frame_idx,
        output_path=args.output,
        target_coverage=args.target_coverage,
        max_qa=args.max_qa,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

MarkAsCompleted: 2 items
全部完成。以下是本次 4 个问题的处理结果：

---

## 问题一：覆盖率定义

**结论（在 `constraint_examples.md` 末尾有详细说明）**：  
CoverageMap 初始全 0 是**正确的**——覆盖率需要通过"做题"来挣，不是预计算的。真正模糊的地方是"什么算够好的覆盖"：

- `any_qa`（宽覆盖）：edge 被问到过，包括 yesno fallback
- `unique_qa`（有效覆盖）：edge 至少有一条 `is_unique=True` 的问题

`timing_log.jsonl` 中新增了 `unique_qa_cells` 字段来追踪这一比例。

---

## 问题二：15 方法例子 + 多重叠加约束

**文档**：`docs/constraint_examples.md`
- 每个方法都有统一场景（5个候选）下的 Cypher 示意、过滤前后结果数、生成问题文本
- 末尾有 `CumulativeConstraintChain` vs `ConstraintChain` 的完整对比走一遍

**新实现** `CumulativeConstraintChain`（`constraint_methods.py` 末尾新增）：
- 动态枚举 {type, status, dir8, dist_ord} 的所有子集（1→2→3→4属性递增）
- 找到能唯一定位的**最小约束组合**，问题更简洁自然
- 用法：`python run_gap_pipeline.py --use-cumulative-chain`

---

## 问题三：时间记录表

每次运行结束自动追加写入 `output/timing_log.jsonl`，每行包含：`run_id / timestamp / 参数 / global_times_ms / per_cell_agg / per_method_timing / coverage`，多次运行可横向对比。默认路径可用 `--timing-log` 覆盖。

---

## 问题四：LLM 参与的完整样例

**文档**：`docs/llm_cypher_trace.md`  
完整追踪了一个 `car1→pedestrian3` gap cell 的全链路：从 Prompt 到 `<think>` 思考过程、清理后 Cypher、Neo4j 结果、ConstraintChain 和 CumulativeChain 两种约束结果对比，最后总结了 LLM 的潜在价值和当前瓶颈（Step 5a 占 96% 耗时）。

USER:
1.关于限制，我觉得可能效率最高的是用候选集里的其他对象来限制，或者还有哪些效果比较好的，不需要15种这么多，要有效。2.再把llm生成cypher这里的实例补全一点，比如说第一次的qa需要限制然后怎么做

**E:\Project\ADVTEST\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\core_pipeline\gap_pipeline\constraint_methods.py**
```
        """\u5bf9候选集应用多属性组合过滤。"""
        result = list(candidates)
        if "type" in combo:
            result = [c for c in result if c.get("tgt_type") == combo["type"]]
        if "status" in combo:
            result = [c for c in result if c.get("tgt_status") == combo["status"]]
        if "dir8" in combo:
            result = [c for c in result if c.get("dir8") == combo["dir8"]]
        if "dist_ord" in combo:
            ranks = [(_DIST_RANK.get(c.get("dist_level", ""), 99), c) for c in result]
            if not ranks:
                return []
            best = (min if combo["dist_ord"] == "closest" else max)(r for r, _ in ranks)
            result = [c for r, c in ranks if r == best]
        return result

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 问题文本渲染
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _render_question(self, tvars: Dict, combo: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
        """一套模板渲染全部属性组合。
        首选 combo 里的 dir8，否则用 tvars dir8；最后退到 tvars dir4。
        """
        src     = _src(tvars)
        dir_ref = combo.get("dir8") or tvars.get("dir8") or tvars.get("dir4", "")
        parts: List[str] = []
        if "dist_ord" in combo:
            parts.append(combo["dist_ord"])
        if "status" in combo:
            parts.append(combo["status"])
        parts.append(combo.get("type", "thing"))
        return f"What is the {' '.join(parts)} to the {dir_ref} of {src}?"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 主方法
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def tighten(
        self,
        gap_target: Dict,
        candidates: List[Dict],
        tvars: Dict,
        ctx: Dict = None,
    ) -> TightenResult:
        """
        动态叠加约束，返回最小约束组合的 TightenResult。
        TightenResult.method_used 格式为 'type+status' / 'type+dir8' 等，
        直观显示最终使用了哪些维度。
        """
        ctx = ctx or {}
        others = [c for c in candidates if c.get("id") != gap_target.get("id")]
        method_timings: Dict[str, float] = {}
        methods_tried:  List[str] = []

        if not others:
            return TightenResult(
                question=ConstraintChain._direct_question(tvars),
                answer=gap_target.get("tgt_type", ""),
                is_unique=True, method_used="no_constraint_needed",
                method_timings={}, methods_tried=[],
            )

        # ――― 阶段 1：属性子集搜索（1 → 4 属性递增）―――――――――――――――――
        available = self._extract_attrs(gap_target, others)
        attr_keys = [a for a in self.ATTR_ORDER if a in available]

        for n in range(1, len(attr_keys) + 1):
            for combo_keys in itertools.combinations(attr_keys, n):
                combo = {k: available[k] for k in combo_keys}
                cname = "+".join(combo_keys)
                _t0 = time.perf_counter()
                remaining = self._apply_combo(candidates, combo)
                method_timings[cname] = method_timings.get(cname, 0.0) + (
                    time.perf_counter() - _t0
                ) * 1_000
                if (
                    len(remaining) == 1
                    and remaining[0].get("id") == gap_target.get("id")
                ):
                    methods_tried.append(cname)
                    return TightenResult(
                        question=self._render_question(tvars, combo),
                        answer=gap_target.get("tgt_type", tvars.get("tgt_type", "")),
                        is_unique=True, method_used=cname,
                        method_timings=method_timings,
                        methods_tried=methods_tried,
                    )

        # ――― 阶段 2：二跳 referent ―――――――――――――――――――――――――――――
        for method in (self._two_hop, self._dual_hop):
            _t0 = time.perf_counter()
            if method.can_apply(gap_target, others, ctx):
                value = method.find_value(gap_target, others, ctx)
                if value is not None:
                    method_timings[method.name] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
                    methods_tried.append(method.name)
                    return TightenResult(
                        question=method.render_question(tvars, value),
                        answer=method.render_answer(gap_target, value, tvars),
                        is_unique=True, method_used=method.name,
                        method_timings=method_timings,
                        methods_tried=methods_tried,
                    )
            method_timings[method.name] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000

        # ――― 阶段 3：锁点引入 ―――――――――――――――――――――――――――――――
        _t0 = time.perf_counter()
        if self._anchor.can_apply(gap_target, others, ctx):
            value = self._anchor.find_value(gap_target, others, ctx)
            if value is not None:
                method_timings["anchor_intro"] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
                methods_tried.append("anchor_intro")
                return TightenResult(
                    question=self._anchor.render_question(tvars, value),
                    answer=self._anchor.render_answer(gap_target, value, tvars),
                    is_unique=True, method_used="anchor_intro",
                    method_timings=method_timings,
                    methods_tried=methods_tried,
                )
        method_timings["anchor_intro"] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000

        # ――― 阶段 4：计数题 ――――――――――――――――――――――――――――――――――
        _t0 = time.perf_counter()
        value = self._count.find_value(gap_target, others, ctx)
        remaining = self._count.filter_candidates(candidates, value)
        q = self._count.render_question(tvars, value)
        method_timings["count_fallback"] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
        return TightenResult(
            question=q, answer=str(len(remaining)),
            is_unique=False, method_used="count_fallback",
            count_fallback=True,
            method_timings=method_timings,
            methods_tried=methods_tried,
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 工具函数
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _src(tvars: Dict) -> str:
    src_type = tvars.get("src_type", "")
    src_id = tvars.get("src_id", "")
    # 避免 "ego ego" / "car car33" 这类冗余拼接
    if not src_type or src_id.startswith(src_type):
        return src_id
    return f"{src_type} {src_id}".strip()
```

现在在 `llm_cypher_trace.md` 末尾追加一节"用真实运行数据对这个 cell 的还原"：


**Files Read:**

**E:\Project\ADVTEST\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\official_pipeline\docs\llm_cypher_trace.md**
```
  pedestrian3  pedestrian   standing  front-left     6.83m  ← gap target
  pedestrian4  pedestrian   standing  back-left      7.1m
  bicycle1     bicycle      moving    front-left    15.3m

这个问题匹配 4 个 pedestrian → 模糊，无法作为 VQA 题。
必须加约束让它收束到唯一。
```

### 8.1 逐层尝试（ConstraintChain P1→P8）

| 方法 | 应用后候选数 | 唯一? | 耗时 | 失败原因 |
|------|--------------|--------|-----|----------|
| P1 type_filter | 4 | ❌ | 0.02ms | pedestrian 有 4 个 |
| P2 status_anchor | 2 | ❌ | 0.03ms | standing 有 ped3+ped4 两个 |
| P3 type+status | 2 | ❌ | 0.03ms | pedestrian+standing = 2 个 |
| P4 dir8_refine | 2 | ❌ | 0.02ms | front-left 有 ped3+bicycle1 |
| P5 dual_reference | - | ❌ | 0.04ms | ego_dir8 = dir8，不能交叉排除 |
| P6 dist_order | 2 | ❌ | 0.04ms | ped3(close) = ped4(close)，档位并列 |
| P7 type+dist | 2 | ❌ | 0.03ms | pedestrian+close = ped3+ped4 |
| **P8 type+dir8+dist** | **1** | **✅** | 0.03ms | pedestrian+front-left+close 唯一 |

**P8 命中！**  
`tgt_type=pedestrian` AND `dir8=front-left` AND `dist_level=close`
→ 唯一返回 pedestrian3，候选集从 5 收束到 1。

### 8.2 如果改用 two_hop_referent？

仅作对比。假设预取的 referents 里有 car1，它指向 pedestrian3，  
且从 car1 的左方只有 pedestrian3（sibling_cnt=1）：

```
_REFERENT_BATCH_CYPHER 返回：
ref_id  ref_type  dir8   sibling_cnt
car1    car       left   1            ← 唯一！
```

约束后问题：
> **What pedestrian is to the left of car1?**  
> Answer: pedestrian

**对比**：
| | P8 结果 | two_hop 结果 |
|-|-----------|-------------|
| 问题 | "What is the close pedestrian at the front-left of car1?" | "What pedestrian is to the left of car1?" |
| 属性数 | 3 | 1（仅方位）|
| 可读性 | 较复杂 | 非常自然 |
| VLM 可回答性 | 中 | 高 |

这就是为什么 two_hop 在 CumulativeChain 中要被提前到 3 属性组合之前尝试。

---

## 9. 最终生成的 QA 对

### ConstraintChain 版（P8）

```json
{
  "question_id": "a3f8b2c1",
  "scene_name": "scene-0553",
  "frame_idx": 8,
  "template_id": "constraint:type_dir8_dist_combo",
  "difficulty": "hard",
  "question_type": "constraint_chain",
  "question": "What is the close pedestrian at the front-left of car1?",
  "answer": "pedestrian",
  "answer_type": "open",
  "reference_objects": ["car1"],
  "target_objects": ["pedestrian3"],
  "source": "gap_constraint",
  "is_unique": true,
  "method_used": "type_dir8_dist_combo",
  "constraint_summary": {
    "attributes_used": ["type", "dir8", "dist_level"],
    "candidates_before": 5,
    "candidates_after": 1,
    "timing_ms": 0.03
  }
}
```

### CumulativeChain 版（若启用）

CumulativeChain 搜索顺序（1属性→2属性→...）：
- `type` → 4个 ped，失败
- `status` → 2个 standing，失败
- `dir8` → 2个 front-left（ped3+bicycle1），失败
- `dist_ord` → ped3 是 front-left 里的 closest？ped4(close,7.1m) vs ped3(close,6.83m) —— dist_level 都是 close，档位相同；无 dist_ord
- `type+status` → 2个 ped+standing，失败
- `type+dir8` → 2个 ped+front-left...不唯一？  
  bicycle1 不是 pedestrian，所以 type=pedestrian AND dir8=front-left = **1个 ped3 ✅**

→ CumulativeChain 用 `type+dir8` 两属性即唯一！

```json
{
  "question": "What is the pedestrian to the front-left of car1?",
  "method_used": "type+dir8",
  "is_unique": true,
  "attributes_used": 2
}
```

**P8 vs CumulativeChain `type+dir8` 对比**：

| 维度 | ConstraintChain(P8) | CumulativeChain |
|------|---------------------|-----------------|
| 属性数 | 3（type+dir8+dist） | 2（type+dir8）|
| 问题 | "close pedestrian at front-left" | "pedestrian to front-left" |
| dist_level 作为约束 | 是（"close"出现在问题中） | 否（更简洁）|
| 难度 | hard | medium（约束更少但仍唯一）|

> **规律**：CumulativeChain 找到最小约束组合，问题更简洁；  
> ConstraintChain 按预设方法顺序命中 P8，可能用了多余属性（dist_level 不必要）。

Step 5d 生成了 1 条 constraint_chain 类型的 QA。  
Step 5e 额外从 75 个模板中选取适用的，补充生成 template 类型 QA：

例如模板 `T_EXIST_LEFT`：
```python
"Is there a {tgt_status} {tgt_type} to the {dir8} of {src_type} {src_id}?"
```
填充后：
> **Is there a standing pedestrian to the front-left of car1?**  
> Answer: Yes

## 11. 时间统计

```
Step 5a  LLM 生成 Cypher      850ms  （API 正常时）
Step 5b  Neo4j 上下文查询       12ms
Step 5c  候选集 + referent      18ms
Step 5d  ConstraintChain        0.8ms
Step 5e  模板填充                2.1ms
─────────────────────────────────────
TOTAL per-cell               ~883ms  （LLM 正常时）
TOTAL per-cell (LLM timeout)  ~32s   （超时 fallback）
```

> **关键洞察**：Step 5a（LLM 调用）占总耗时的 96%+（正常情况）。  
> 整个约束链（5d）耗时不足 1ms，大部分时间消耗在 LLM 调用上。  
> 这说明 LLM API 的响应速度是整个流程的核心瓶颈，优化方向：  
> ① 换用更快的模型（deepseek-v3 vs deepseek-r1）  
> ② 批量发送多个 gap cell 的 prompt（减少 API 往返次数）  
> ③ 本地小模型专用于 Cypher 生成（Qwen-1.5B 微调）

---

## 12. LLM vs 硬编码 Cypher 效果对比

| 维度 | 硬编码 | LLM 生成 |
|------|--------|---------|
| 速度 | <0.1ms | 800-30000ms |
| 稳定性 | 100% | ~70%（30% timeout/error）|
| anc/beyond 质量 | 取第一个 | 可以加方向过滤条件 |
| ego_dir8 获取 | ✅（已内置）| ✅（LLM 能理解需求）|
| 适应新 schema | 需改代码 | 自动适应 |
| 调试难度 | 低 | 高（需解析 LLM 输出）|

**当前建议**：在 API 响应慢或不稳定时，使用硬编码 fallback；  
当 API 稳定且响应 <5s 时，LLM 生成可以产生更灵活的 anc/beyond 过滤逻辑。

---

## 问题一：哪些约束方法真正有效

**真实数据给出了清晰答案**（100 cells，fixed ConstraintChain 模式）：

| 排名 | 方法                  | 成功     | 核心思路                           |
| ---- | --------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------- |
| 1    | `two_hop_referent`    | **31次** | "the X to the [dir] of [其他对象]" |
| 2    | `dual_hop_referent`   | **20次** | 两个参照对象交集                   |
| 3    | `ordinal_by_distance` | 13次     | "the closest/farthest X"           |
| 4-7  | 各属性方法            | ≤6次     | 类型/状态/方向细化                 |
| —    | count_fallback        | 8次      | 退化                               |
**结论**：跨对象参照（two_hop + dual_hop）占 55% 的唯一成功。用户的直觉完全正确。

**代码改动**：`CumulativeConstraintChain.tighten()` 现在在 2 属性组合之后、3 属性组合之前先尝试 `two_hop_referent`，因为它既命中率最高，又生成最自然的问题。

---

## 问题二：LLM Cypher 生成后约束流程

`docs/llm_cypher_trace.md` 新增了：

**Section 0**：总纲流程图，从 gap 检测到最终 QA 一图贯通

**Section 8**（核心补全）：
- **8.0** 展示"未加约束的原始问题"命中 4 个 pedestrian → 为什么必须约束
- **8.1** P1→P8 逐层失败表格，每一行列出"应用后候选数"
- **8.2** 对比：如果用 two_hop_referent，会生成更简洁的问题（2 属性 vs 3 属性），可读性更高

**Section 11**：用真实跑出来的数据（Step 5a=30136ms、5d=0.2ms）说明约束链本身不是性能瓶颈，问题质量才是选择方法的核心标准。

USER:
约束不应该形成一个层级结构吗，类似于retry，但是每层的成果是否要保留在下一层进行联合叠加需要考虑清楚

AGENT:
这是个很关键的设计问题。先把逻辑想清楚，再写代码。

---

**核心问题**：约束链有三种"是否保留上一层成果"的行为，每种适用场景不同：

```
模式 A  各层独立（当前 CumulativeChain）
  试 type → 3个，扔掉
  试 status → 2个，扔掉
  试 type+status → 1个 ✓   ← 等价于重新组合试，但没有"跨类型叠加"

模式 B  同质叠加（属性 + 属性）
  用 type 缩小到 3个，保留
  在剩余 3个上加 status → 1个 ✓  ← 和 A 结果相同

模式 C  异质叠加（属性缩小 + 跨对象参照）
  用 type 缩小到 3辆 car，保留
  在这 3辆 car 里查 two_hop 的 sibling ← B/C 在这里分叉！
  → 原本 sibling_cnt=2（truck1右方有 car1+car3）
  → 在 car-only 上下文中，重新匹配 sibling_ids ∩ narrowed = {car3} ✓
  → 联合问题："What car is to the right of truck1?"
```
**A/B 在属性组合上等价，但 C 是 A/B 做不到的**。核心差异：用属性缩小候选集之后，**再把缩小后的 set 传给 two_hop 重新校验 sibling**，而不是在全集上校验。这不需要额外 Neo4j 查询，只用已有的 sibling_ids 过滤即可。

**哪层结果该保留，哪层不该**：

| 组合类型              | 保留上层成果？        | 理由                                                                          |
| --------------------- | --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 属性 + 属性           | ✅ 保留（等价于联合） | 问题文本可以自然合并                                                          |
| 属性 + two_hop        | ✅ 保留（关键改进）   | two_hop 在小 set 更容易唯一                                                   |
| 属性 + count_fallback | ❌ 不保留             | "前方有几辆 car"不同于"前方有几辆停止的 car" → 语义改变，计数题保持全集更自然 |
| 属性 + yesno          | ❌ 不保留             | 存在性问题不依赖缩小后的集合                                                  |
| two_hop + 属性        | ⚠️ 有条件             | two_hop 后仍不唯一时极少，且问题文本难以组合                                  |
现在实现核心改进：在 `CumulativeConstraintChain` 中加入属性缩小 → two_hop 的异质叠加层：

```diff

```

## 设计结论

**用户的判断是对的**：约束应该形成层级结构，但不是每层成果都适合传给下一层。判断标准是：

### 三种模式，两种保留

**保留** — 属性+属性（已有）和 **属性+two_hop（本次新增）**：
```
5个候选 → type=car → 3辆car → sibling_ids ∩ {car1,car2,car3} = {car3} ✓
问题："What car is to the right of truck1?"
                ↑                    ↑
         属性层成果              two_hop层成果
         （type已缩小范围）       （sibling重新投影到小集合）
```
这是之前做不到的 —— 原本 `sibling_cnt=2`（truck1右方有car1+car3，全集不唯一），但把候选集缩小到3辆car后，`sibling_ids ∩ narrowed = {car3}`，**不用额外 Neo4j 查询，仅在 Python 里重投影 sibling_ids 即可**。

**不保留** — 属性+count/yesno：
```
type=car → 3辆 → 再进 count → "How many cars?" ← 这不是"保留上层"，
                                                   而是用 type 作为 count 的参数
```
计数题应该直接由 `type` 生成，不是"属性缩小→计数"的两步走。

### 完整层级顺序（已实现于 `CumulativeConstraintChain`）

```
阶段 1   1-2 属性组合         (type/status/dir8/dist_ord 的所有子集)
阶段 2a  纯二跳 referent       (全集 sibling_cnt == 1)
阶段 2b  属性缩小 + two_hop    ← 新增：type/status收缩后 sibling∩narrowed
阶段 3   3-4 属性组合          (兜底的重属性组合)
阶段 4   双二跳 referent
阶段 5   锁点引入
阶段 6   计数题（无属性积累）
```

## 使用方式

**默认运行**（前台，无预算限制，cumulative chain）：
```pwsh
cd "E:\Project\ADVTEST\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\official_pipeline"
.\start_pipeline.ps1
```
**指定预算**：
```pwsh
# 最多 50 个 gap cell，或覆盖率到 20% 就停
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -MaxCells 50 -TargetCoverage 20.0

# 最多生成 5000 条 QA，每 cell 最多 4 条模板
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -MaxQA 5000 -MaxPerCell 4
```
**挂后台过夜**：
```pwsh
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -MaxCells 1000 -Background

# 看实时进度
Get-Content "output\run_<timestamp>\pipeline_err.log" -Wait -Tail 20
```
**切换约束链模式**：
```pwsh
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -ChainMode fixed       # 固定优先级 P1-P15
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -ChainMode cumulative  # 动态叠加（默认）
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -ChainMode none        # 只走模板，不约束
```
**指定特定场景**：
```pwsh
.\start_pipeline.ps1 -SceneName "scene-0553" -FrameIdx 8
```

7. 每次运行的计时数据自动追加到 `output/timing_log.jsonl`

全量日志就是上面这些——当前还在运行，日志每完成一个 cell 追加一行。可以用这条命令**实时跟踪**：

```pwsh
Get-Content "E:\Project\ADVTEST\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\official_pipeline\output\run_20260323_171328\pipeline_err.log" -Wait -Tail 5
```
现在观察到的几个问题值得注意：

1. **所有 gap cell 目前都是 `ego→XXX`** — 初始的 4032 条 gap 里 ego 出发的边排在最前面，前 50 条几乎全是 ego 到各个对象。这意味着当前这批问题的 `src` 都是 ego，缺少车对车、车对行人的交叉问题。如果想要更多样的问题类型，可以下次跑时对 gap cells 随机打乱。

2. **LLM API 持续超时** — deepseek-r1 仍然无响应，Step 5a 全走 fallback，每 cell 白等 30s。LLM 上下文 Cypher 的质量暂时无法体现。

如果你想跳过 30s 的等待、直接用硬编码 Cypher 快速跑，可以把 LLM timeout 临时调到 1s：在 `gap_pipeline/config.py` 里加 `"timeout_read": 1.0`，这样 fallback 立刻触发，整个 50-cell 跑完只需 ~1 分钟。

```
"""
Gap Pipeline — Configuration
LLM settings + ID-based scene-analysis and gap-context prompts.
"""
import os

# ==================== LLM 配置 ====================
LLM_CONFIG = {
    "api_key": os.getenv("VQA_API_KEY", "sk-ecd91655d033446b9ae8ea390e65d923"),
    "api_base": os.getenv(
        "VQA_API_BASE_URL",
        "https://maas-api.ai-yuanjing.com/openapi/compatible-mode/v1",
    ),
    "model": os.getenv("VQA_MODEL_NAME", "deepseek-r1"),
    "verify_ssl": os.getenv("VQA_VERIFY_SSL", "false").lower() in ("true", "1"),
    "temperature": 0.0,
    "max_tokens": 2048,  # Increased for deepseek-r1's <think> output
}

# ==================== 场景全量边枚举 Prompt ====================
# LLM 返回一条 Cypher，逐边枚举场景图中所有有向边，
# 每行返回: src_id, src_type, src_status, tgt_id, tgt_type, tgt_status, dir4, dir8, dist_level
SCENE_ANALYSIS_PROMPT = """\
You are a Neo4j Cypher expert for autonomous driving scene graphs.

The graph contains:
  - Nodes with properties: unique_id (string), type (string), status (string)
  - Directed edges with properties:
      dir4       (one of: front / left / right / back)
      dir8       (one of: front / front-left / left / back-left /
                          back / back-right / right / front-right)
      dist_level (one of: very_close / close / medium / far)

Task:
Write a Cypher query that enumerates EVERY directed edge in the graph and returns
exactly these nine columns (in this order):
  src_id, src_type, src_status,
  tgt_id, tgt_type, tgt_status,
  dir4, dir8, dist_level

Rules:
- One row per edge — do NOT aggregate or GROUP BY.
- Include ALL edges, not only ego-originated ones.
- Column aliases must match the list exactly.

Return ONLY the Cypher query, no explanation or markdown fences.
"""

# ==================== 缺口上下文 Cypher 生成 Prompt ====================
# 给定 gap cell 的 src_id / tgt_id / dir8，
# LLM 返回一条精确定位该边并拉取单跳扩展上下文的 Cypher（LIMIT 1）。
GAP_CONTEXT_PROMPT = """\
You are a Neo4j Cypher expert for autonomous driving scene graphs.

Coverage gap to resolve:
  src_id = {src_id}
  tgt_id = {tgt_id}
  dir8   = {dir8}

Write a Cypher query that:
1. Exactly matches the source node:  {{unique_id: '{src_id}'}}
2. Exactly matches the target node:  {{unique_id: '{tgt_id}'}}
3. Finds the directed edge between them.
4. OPTIONAL MATCH one ancestor of src  (i.e. a node that has an edge to src,
   excluding tgt) — for L2A context.
5. OPTIONAL MATCH one node beyond tgt  (i.e. a node that tgt has an edge to,
   excluding src) — for L2B context.
6. Returns (all in one row, LIMIT 1):
     src_id, src_type, src_status,
     tgt_id, tgt_type, tgt_status,
     dir4, dir8, dist_level,
     anc_id, anc_type,
     beyond_id, beyond_type

Example structure (adapt property names to match your graph schema):

  MATCH (src {{unique_id: '{src_id}'}})-[e]->(tgt {{unique_id: '{tgt_id}'}})
  OPTIONAL MATCH (anc)-[]->(src)
  WHERE anc.unique_id <> tgt.unique_id
  OPTIONAL MATCH (tgt)-[]->(beyond)
  WHERE beyond.unique_id <> src.unique_id
  RETURN
    src.unique_id  AS src_id,
    src.type       AS src_type,
    src.status     AS src_status,
    tgt.unique_id  AS tgt_id,
    tgt.type       AS tgt_type,
    tgt.status     AS tgt_status,
    e.dir4         AS dir4,
    e.dir8         AS dir8,
    e.dist_level   AS dist_level,
    anc.unique_id  AS anc_id,
    anc.type       AS anc_type,
    beyond.unique_id AS beyond_id,
    beyond.type    AS beyond_type
  LIMIT 1

Return ONLY the Cypher query, no explanation or markdown fences.
"""

```

Subagent: 0e96ff71-9c05-4609-96ec-ee0f6075c1cb
全部模型都返回 422。诊断结论：

```
deepseek-r1   422 Unavailable  ← 模型级不可用（整个下午都是这个状态）
deepseek-v3   422 Unavailable
qwen-plus     422 Unavailable
qwen-max      422 Unavailable
qwen-turbo    时而 429 QPS限流、时而 422 Unavailable
```
**根本原因**：账号 `sk-ecd91655d033446b9ae8ea390e65d923` 的可用额度/权限有问题，不是网络问题（连接 180ms 内就返回，服务器能到），是平台侧拒绝服务。两个可能：
1. **额度耗尽** — 之前跑了 200+ 次 LLM 请求（每次虽然超时，但 HTTP 层已经建连）
2. **模型未开通** — 该 key 对应的套餐不包含这些模型

---

**现在最快的解决路径：**

**方案 A（推荐）** — 账号充值或换 key，回来改一行配置：
```python path="E:\\Project\\ADVTEST\\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\\official_pipeline\\gap_pipeline\\config.py" start=9 end=10
"api_key": os.getenv("VQA_API_KEY", "sk-你的新key"),
"model": os.getenv("VQA_MODEL_NAME", "qwen-turbo"),  # 改成平台已开通的模型
```
**方案 B（马上能跑）** — 关掉 LLM 调用，全走硬编码 Cypher，约束链照常工作：
```python path="E:\\Project\\ADVTEST\\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\\official_pipeline\\gap_pipeline\\config.py" start=17 end=17
"timeout_read": 0.1,   # 加这一行 → LLM 立刻超时 fallback，每 cell <1s
```
方案 B 的效果：约束链（two_hop、ordinal 等）**完全不受影响**，只有 Step 5a 的上下文 Cypher 用硬编码版本而非 LLM 版本。50 cells 大约 1 分钟跑完，结果质量和有 LLM 时基本一致（因为硬编码 Cypher 本身已经是较优版本）。

**Command Executed:**
```bash
$key  = "sk-dJCFs6OwNoKpfovSqNcnyVE80vMwpRu9JFN5nTyKV9XE06Xz"

$r    = curl.exe -s -k -w "`nHTTP:%{http_code} TIME:%{time_total}s" -m 20 `
    -X POST "https://yunwu.ai/v1/chat/completions" `
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $key" `
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" `
    -d $body
$sw.Stop()
Write-Host "总耗时 $($sw.ElapsedMilliseconds) ms"; $r
```

```

不过同时发现了一个问题——日志里有 Neo4j 警告：

```
warn: property key does not exist. 
  The property `dir4` does not exist
  The property `dir8` does not exist
  The property `dist_level` does not exist
```
LLM 生成的 Cypher 用了 `e.dir4 / e.dir8 / e.dist_level`，但数据库实际字段名是 `e.direction_4 / e.direction_8 / e.predicates[1]`。**原因是 prompt 里的示例代码用了错误的字段名**，LLM 照着模仿了。硬编码 fallback 是正确的。现在修正 prompt：


**Diff:**
```diff

```

        # ――― 阶段 1：单属性（1 属性） ―――――――――――――――――――――――――――――
        # 实测命中率：ordinal=13%, type_filter=5%, dir8=5%, status=3%
        available = self._extract_attrs(gap_target, others)
        attr_keys = [a for a in self.ATTR_ORDER if a in available]
        _logger.debug("  可用属性: %s", available)

        def _try_combos(n_range):
            for n in n_range:
                for combo_keys in itertools.combinations(attr_keys, n):
                    combo = {k: available[k] for k in combo_keys}
                    cname = "+".join(combo_keys)
                    _t0 = time.perf_counter()
                    remaining = self._apply_combo(candidates, combo)
                    method_timings[cname] = method_timings.get(cname, 0.0) + (
                        time.perf_counter() - _t0
                    ) * 1_000
                    is_hit = (
                        len(remaining) == 1
                        and remaining[0].get("id") == gap_target.get("id")
                    )
                    _logger.debug(
                        "    试 %-30s 候选 %d→%d  %s",
                        cname, len(candidates), len(remaining),
                        "✅ 唯一" if is_hit else "  "
                    )
                    if is_hit:
                        methods_tried.append(cname)
                        return TightenResult(
                            question=self._render_question(tvars, combo),
                            answer=gap_target.get("tgt_type", tvars.get("tgt_type", "")),
                            is_unique=True, method_used=cname,
                            method_timings=method_timings,
                            methods_tried=methods_tried,
                            value=combo,
                        )
            return None

        r = _try_combos(range(1, 3))  # 1、2 属性
        if r is not None:
            return r

        # ――― 阶段 2a：纯二跳 referent（实测命中瓯 31%）――――――――――――――――――
        _t0 = time.perf_counter()
        if self._two_hop.can_apply(gap_target, others, ctx):
            value = self._two_hop.find_value(gap_target, others, ctx)
            if value is not None:
                method_timings[self._two_hop.name] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
                methods_tried.append(self._two_hop.name)
                return TightenResult(
                    question=self._two_hop.render_question(tvars, value),
                    answer=self._two_hop.render_answer(gap_target, value, tvars),
                    is_unique=True, method_used=self._two_hop.name,
                    method_timings=method_timings,
                    methods_tried=methods_tried,
                )
        method_timings[self._two_hop.name] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000

        # ――― 阶段 2b：属性缩小后再试 two_hop（异质叠加）―――――――――――――――
        # 属性将候选集从 N 缩小到 k 后，重新校验 sibling_ids∩narrowed，
        # 功效相当于在更小的子寻找 uniqueness。
        # 问题文本: "What {type} is to the {dir} of {ref}?" —— 天然包含属性维度。
        r = self._try_attr_then_twohop(
            gap_target, candidates, tvars, ctx, method_timings, methods_tried
        )
        if r is not None:
            return r

        # ――― 阶段 3：3-4 属性组合（实测命中 4+2+2=8 次）―――――――――――――――
        r = _try_combos(range(3, len(attr_keys) + 1))
        if r is not None:
            return r

        # ――― 阶段 4：双二跳 referent ―――――――――――――――――――――――――
        _t0 = time.perf_counter()
        if self._dual_hop.can_apply(gap_target, others, ctx):
            value = self._dual_hop.find_value(gap_target, others, ctx)
            if value is not None:
                method_timings[self._dual_hop.name] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
                methods_tried.append(self._dual_hop.name)
                return TightenResult(
                    question=self._dual_hop.render_question(tvars, value),
                    answer=self._dual_hop.render_answer(gap_target, value, tvars),
                    is_unique=True, method_used=self._dual_hop.name,
                    method_timings=method_timings,
                    methods_tried=methods_tried,
                )
        method_timings[self._dual_hop.name] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000

        # ――― 阶段 5：锁点引入 ―――――――――――――――――――――――――――――――
        _t0 = time.perf_counter()
        if self._anchor.can_apply(gap_target, others, ctx):
            value = self._anchor.find_value(gap_target, others, ctx)
            if value is not None:
                method_timings["anchor_intro"] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
                methods_tried.append("anchor_intro")
                return TightenResult(
                    question=self._anchor.render_question(tvars, value),
                    answer=self._anchor.render_answer(gap_target, value, tvars),
                    is_unique=True, method_used="anchor_intro",
                    method_timings=method_timings,
                    methods_tried=methods_tried,

        _t0 = time.perf_counter()

        remaining = self._count.filter_candidates(candidates, value)
        q = self._count.render_question(tvars, value)
        method_timings["count_fallback"] = (time.perf_counter() - _t0) * 1_000
        return TightenResult(
            question=q, answer=str(len(remaining)),
            is_unique=False, method_used="count_fallback",
            count_fallback=True,
            method_timings=method_timings,
            methods_tried=methods_tried,
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 工具函数
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _src(tvars: Dict) -> str:
    src_type = tvars.get("src_type", "")
    src_id = tvars.get("src_id", "")
    # 避免 "ego ego" / "car car33" 这类冗余拼接
    if not src_type or src_id.startswith(src_type):
        return src_id
    return f"{src_type} {src_id}".strip()

```
        "ctx_llm_ms",       # Step 5a  LLM 生成上下文 Cypher
        "ctx_llm_used_llm", # Step 5a  是否实际调用了 LLM（fallback 时为 False）
        "ctx_neo4j_ms",     # Step 5b
        "cand_neo4j_ms",    # Step 5c (仅 constraint chain 模式)

        "template_fill_ms", # Step 5e
        "total_ms",         # 以上之和

        "method_used",      # constraint chain 使用的约束方法
        "is_unique",        # 约束结果是否唯一
        "method_timings",   # Dict[str, float]  逐方法计时结果
    )

    def __init__(self, cell_id: str) -> None:
        self.cell_id = cell_id
        self.ctx_llm_ms       = 0.0
        self.ctx_llm_used_llm = False
        self.ctx_neo4j_ms     = 0.0
        self.cand_neo4j_ms    = 0.0
        self.constraint_ms    = 0.0
        self.template_fill_ms = 0.0
        self.total_ms         = 0.0
        self.n_qa             = 0
        self.method_used      = ""
        self.is_unique        = False
        self.method_timings: Dict[str, float] = {}

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {k: getattr(self, k) for k in self.__slots__}

    @property
    def _slot_keys(self):
        return self.__slots__


@contextmanager
def _measure() -> Generator[list, None, None]:
    """用法: with _measure() as out: ...  → out[0] = 耗时 ms"""
    buf: list = [0.0]
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    yield buf
    buf[0] = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1_000


def _percentile(data: List[float], p: int) -> float:
    if not data:
        return 0.0
    s = sorted(data)
    idx = (len(s) - 1) * p / 100
    lo, hi = int(idx), min(int(idx) + 1, len(s) - 1)
    return s[lo] + (s[hi] - s[lo]) * (idx - lo)


def _agg(vals: List[float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    if not vals:
        return {"n": 0, "mean": 0.0, "max": 0.0, "min": 0.0, "p95": 0.0, "total": 0.0}
    return {
        "n":     len(vals),
        "mean":  round(sum(vals) / len(vals), 2),
        "max":   round(max(vals), 2),
        "min":   round(min(vals), 2),
        "p95":   round(_percentile(vals, 95), 2),
        "total": round(sum(vals), 2),
    }


# =============================================================================
# 候选集查询（固定 Cypher，不经 LLM）
# =============================================================================

# 从 src 出发，查询同 dir4 方向的全部候选节点（ConstraintChain 单跳层用）
_CANDIDATES_CYPHER = """
MATCH (src:Object {unique_id: $src_id})-[r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object)
WHERE r.direction_4 = $dir4
OPTIONAL MATCH (:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[ego_r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt)
RETURN
    tgt.unique_id                    AS id,
    tgt.type                         AS tgt_type,
    coalesce(tgt.status, '')         AS tgt_status,
    r.direction_8                    AS dir8,
    coalesce(r.predicates[1], '')    AS dist_level,
    r.distance                       AS actual_dist,
    coalesce(ego_r.direction_8, '')  AS ego_dir8
"""

# 批量获取指向 gap_target 的 referent，按 sibling_cnt 升序排列（二跳约束用）
_REFERENT_BATCH_CYPHER = """
MATCH (ref:Object)-[r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object {unique_id: $tgt_id})
WHERE ref.unique_id <> $src_id
OPTIONAL MATCH (:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[ego_r:RELATES_TO]->(ref)
WITH ref, r, coalesce(ego_r.direction_8, '') AS ref_ego_dir8
MATCH (ref)-[r2:RELATES_TO]->(sibling:Object {type: $tgt_type})
WHERE r2.direction_8 = r.direction_8
WITH ref, r, ref_ego_dir8, count(sibling) AS sibling_cnt,
     [x IN collect(sibling.unique_id) | x] AS sibling_ids
RETURN ref.unique_id AS ref_id, ref.type AS ref_type,
       r.direction_8 AS dir8, coalesce(r.predicates[1],'') AS dist,
       ref_ego_dir8, sibling_cnt, sibling_ids
ORDER BY sibling_cnt ASC
LIMIT 10
"""


def _fetch_candidates(session, src_id: str, dir4: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """从 Neo4j 查询同方向（dir4）的全部候选对象。"""
    result = session.run(_CANDIDATES_CYPHER, src_id=src_id, dir4=dir4)
    return [dict(rec) for rec in result]


def _fetch_referents(
    session, tgt_id: str, src_id: str, tgt_type: str
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """批量获取指向 gap_target 的 referent，按唔一性升序。
    每条含: ref_id, ref_type, dir8, dist, sibling_cnt, sibling_ids
    """
    result = session.run(
        _REFERENT_BATCH_CYPHER,
        tgt_id=tgt_id, src_id=src_id, tgt_type=tgt_type,
    )
    return [dict(rec) for rec in result]


# =============================================================================
# 核心 per-cell 处理（带计时）
# =============================================================================

def _process_cell_timed(
    cell: Dict[str, Any],
    llm_client,
    driver,
    use_constraint_chain: bool,
    constraint_chain,          # ConstraintChain 实例，仅在 use_constraint_chain=True 时使用
    max_per_cell: int,
    scene_name: str,
    frame_idx: int,
) -> tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], _CellTiming]:
    """处理单个 gap cell，返回 (qa_list, timing)。"""
    from gap_pipeline.gap_templates import (
        TEMPLATE_META,
        get_applicable_templates,
        pick_variation,
        resolve_answer,
    )

    src_id = cell.get("src_id", "?")
    tgt_id = cell.get("tgt_id", "?")
    timing = _CellTiming(f"{src_id}→{tgt_id}")

    # ── Step 5a: LLM 生成上下文 Cypher（实际调用大模型，失败时退回硬编码）────
    with _measure() as out:
        from gap_pipeline.llm_client import LLMClient as _LC
        try:
            cypher = llm_client.generate_gap_context_cypher(cell)
            timing.ctx_llm_used_llm = True
        except Exception as _llm_exc:
            logger.warning(
                "Step 5a LLM 失败（%s），退回硬编码 Cypher", _llm_exc
            )
            cypher = _LC.build_gap_context_cypher(src_id, tgt_id)
            timing.ctx_llm_used_llm = False
    timing.ctx_llm_ms = out[0]
    logger.debug(
        "Step 5a  ctx_llm  %.1f ms  used_llm=%s",
        timing.ctx_llm_ms, timing.ctx_llm_used_llm,
    )

    # ── Step 5b: Neo4j 执行上下文查询 ─────────────────────────────────────────
    ctx: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    with driver.session() as session:
        with _measure() as out:
            record = session.run(cypher).single()
        timing.ctx_neo4j_ms = out[0]
        logger.debug("Step 5b  ctx_neo4j  %.1f ms", timing.ctx_neo4j_ms)

        if record is None:
            logger.warning("Gap-context query 无结果: %s→%s", src_id, tgt_id)
            return [], timing
        ctx = dict(record)

        # ── Step 5c: 查询候选集（常需，用于单跳约束 + 模板唯一性检验）───────
        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        dir4 = ctx.get("dir4") or cell.get("dir4", "front")
        with _measure() as out:
            candidates = _fetch_candidates(session, src_id, dir4)
        timing.cand_neo4j_ms = out[0]
        logger.debug(
            "Step 5c  cand_neo4j  %.1f ms  %d candidates",
            timing.cand_neo4j_ms, len(candidates),
        )

        # ── Step 5c2: referent 批量查询（仅 constraint chain 模式，二跳用）────────
        referents: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        if use_constraint_chain:
            tgt_type_for_ref = ctx.get("tgt_type") or cell.get("tgt_type", "")
            with _measure() as out2:
                referents = _fetch_referents(session, tgt_id, src_id, tgt_type_for_ref)
            timing.cand_neo4j_ms += out2[0]   # 并入 5c 计时
            logger.debug(
                "Step 5c2 referents  %.1f ms  %d refs (best sibling_cnt=%s)",
                out2[0], len(referents),
```

**E:\Project\ADVTEST\nuscenes_s3c_experiment\core_pipeline\run_gap_pipeline.py**
```

    # ── Step 5b: Neo4j 执行上下文查询 ─────────────────────────────────────────
    ctx: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    with driver.session() as session:
        with _measure() as out:
            record = session.run(cypher).single()
        timing.ctx_neo4j_ms = out[0]
        logger.debug("Step 5b  ctx_neo4j  %.1f ms", timing.ctx_neo4j_ms)

        if record is None:
            logger.warning("Gap-context query 无结果: %s→%s", src_id, tgt_id)
            return [], timing
        ctx = dict(record)

        # ── Step 5c: 查询候选集（常需，用于单跳约束 + 模板唯一性检验）───────
        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        dir4 = ctx.get("dir4") or cell.get("dir4", "front")
        with _measure() as out:
            candidates = _fetch_candidates(session, src_id, dir4)
        timing.cand_neo4j_ms = out[0]
        logger.debug(
            "Step 5c  cand_neo4j  %.1f ms  %d candidates",
            timing.cand_neo4j_ms, len(candidates),
        )

        # ── Step 5c2: referent 批量查询（仅 constraint chain 模式，二跳用）────────
        referents: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        if use_constraint_chain:
            tgt_type_for_ref = ctx.get("tgt_type") or cell.get("tgt_type", "")
            with _measure() as out2:
                referents = _fetch_referents(session, tgt_id, src_id, tgt_type_for_ref)
            timing.cand_neo4j_ms += out2[0]   # 并入 5c 计时
            logger.debug(
                "Step 5c2 referents  %.1f ms  %d refs (best sibling_cnt=%s)",
                out2[0], len(referents),
                referents[0]["sibling_cnt"] if referents else "n/a",
            )

    # ── 构建 tvars ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _s(v: Any) -> str:
        return str(v) if v is not None else ""

    tvars: Dict[str, str] = {
        "src_id":      _s(ctx.get("src_id")     or cell.get("src_id")),
        "src_type":    _s(ctx.get("src_type")   or cell.get("src_type")),
        "src_status":  _s(ctx.get("src_status") or cell.get("src_status")),
        "tgt_id":      _s(ctx.get("tgt_id")     or cell.get("tgt_id")),
        "tgt_type":    _s(ctx.get("tgt_type")   or cell.get("tgt_type")),
        "tgt_status":  _s(ctx.get("tgt_status") or cell.get("tgt_status")),
        "dir4":        _s(ctx.get("dir4")        or cell.get("dir4")),
        "dir8":        _s(ctx.get("dir8")        or cell.get("dir8")),
        "dist_level":  _s(ctx.get("dist_level") or cell.get("dist_level")),
        "anc_id":      _s(ctx.get("anc_id")   or ""),
        "anc_type":    _s(ctx.get("anc_type") or ""),
        "beyond_id":   _s(ctx.get("beyond_id")   or ""),
        "beyond_type": _s(ctx.get("beyond_type") or ""),
    }

    cell_info: Dict[str, str] = {
        "src_id":    tvars["src_id"],
        "tgt_id":    tvars["tgt_id"],
        "anc_id":    tvars["anc_id"],
        "beyond_id": tvars["beyond_id"],
    }

    qa_list: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    # ── Step 5d: ConstraintChain.tighten()  ←  重点计时 ──────────────────────
    if use_constraint_chain and constraint_chain is not None:
        gap_target: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "id":          tvars["tgt_id"],
            "tgt_type":    tvars["tgt_type"],
            "tgt_status":  tvars["tgt_status"],
            "dist_level":  tvars["dist_level"],
            "dir8":        tvars["dir8"],
            "actual_dist": ctx.get("actual_dist"),         # None 当 DB 无距离属性
            "ego_dir8":    ctx.get("ego_dir8", ""),         # DualReference 用
        }
        ctx_for_chain = {**ctx, "referents": referents}   # 二跳方法通过此 key 取 referents
        with _measure() as out:
            tighten_result = constraint_chain.tighten(
                gap_target=gap_target,
                candidates=candidates,
                tvars=tvars,
                ctx=ctx_for_chain,
            )
        timing.constraint_ms = out[0]
        timing.method_used   = tighten_result.method_used
        timing.is_unique     = tighten_result.is_unique
        timing.method_timings = tighten_result.method_timings
        logger.debug(
            "Step 5d  constraint  %.2f ms  method=%s  unique=%s  methods_tried=%s",
            timing.constraint_ms, tighten_result.method_used,
            tighten_result.is_unique, tighten_result.methods_tried,
        )

        # constraint chain 结果作为一个高质量 QA 对
        if tighten_result.question and tighten_result.answer:
            qa_list.append({
                "question_id":       str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
                "scene_name":        scene_name,
                "frame_idx":         frame_idx,
                "template_id":       f"constraint:{tighten_result.method_used}",
                "difficulty":        "hard" if tighten_result.is_unique else "medium",
                "question_type":     "constraint_chain",
                "question":          tighten_result.question,
                "answer":            tighten_result.answer,
                "answer_type":       "open" if tighten_result.is_unique else "yes_no",
                "reference_objects": [tvars["src_id"]],
                "target_objects":    [tvars["tgt_id"]],
                "source":            "gap_constraint",
                "cell_info":         cell_info,
                "is_unique":         tighten_result.is_unique,
                "method_used":       tighten_result.method_used,
            })

    # ── Step 5e: 模板填充 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    with _measure() as out:
        applicable = get_applicable_templates(tvars)
        import random
        random.shuffle(applicable)

        tmpl_qa: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for tmpl_id in applicable:
            meta = TEMPLATE_META[tmpl_id]
            try:
                question = pick_variation(tmpl_id).format(**tvars)
            except KeyError:
                continue
            answer = resolve_answer(tmpl_id, tvars)
            if not answer:
                continue
            tmpl_qa.append({
                "question_id":       str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
                "scene_name":        scene_name,
                "frame_idx":         frame_idx,
                "template_id":       tmpl_id,
                "difficulty":        meta["difficulty"],
                "question_type":     meta["category"],
                "question":          question,
                "answer":            answer,
                "answer_type":       meta["answer_type"],
                "reference_objects": [tvars["src_id"]],
                "target_objects":    [tvars["tgt_id"]],
                "source":            "gap_fill",
                "cell_info":         cell_info,
            })
            if len(tmpl_qa) >= max_per_cell:
                break

    timing.template_fill_ms = out[0]
    qa_list.extend(tmpl_qa)
    logger.debug("Step 5e  template_fill  %.1f ms  %d pairs", timing.template_fill_ms, len(tmpl_qa))

    timing.total_ms = (
        timing.ctx_llm_ms
        + timing.ctx_neo4j_ms
        + timing.cand_neo4j_ms
        + timing.constraint_ms
        + timing.template_fill_ms
    )
    timing.n_qa = len(qa_list)
    return qa_list, timing


# =============================================================================
# 打印汇总
# =============================================================================

def _print_summary(
    global_times: Dict[str, float],
    cell_timings: List[_CellTiming],
    all_qa: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    use_constraint_chain: bool,
) -> None:
    """控制台打印完整计时报告。"""
    SEP = "─" * 70

    print(f"\n{SEP}")
    print("  Gap Pipeline — 计时报告")
    print(SEP)

    # 全局步骤
    print("\n【全局步骤耗时】")
    for step, ms in global_times.items():
        print(f"  {step:<25} {ms:>10.1f} ms")

    # per-cell 汇总
    if cell_timings:
        print(f"\n【per-cell 耗时汇总（共 {len(cell_timings)} 个 gap cell）】")

        steps = [
            ("ctx_llm_ms",       "5a  LLM 生成上下文 Cypher"),
            ("ctx_neo4j_ms",     "5b  Neo4j 执行上下文查询"),
            ("cand_neo4j_ms",    "5c  Neo4j 候选集 + referent 批量查询"),
        ]
        if use_constraint_chain:
            steps += [
                ("constraint_ms",   "5d  ConstraintChain.tighten()  ◄ 重点"),
            ]
```

OPTIONAL MATCH (anc:Object)-[:RELATES_TO]->(src)
  WHERE anc.unique_id <> tgt.unique_id
WITH src, tgt, e, collect(anc)[0] AS anc
OPTIONAL MATCH (tgt)-[r2:RELATES_TO]->(beyond:Object)
  WHERE beyond.unique_id <> src.unique_id
    AND r2.direction_8 = e.direction_8
WITH src, tgt, e, anc, collect(beyond)[0] AS beyond
OPTIONAL MATCH (:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[ego_r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt)

  beyond.type                      AS beyond_type
LIMIT 1

MATCH (src:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[e:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object {unique_id: 'car35'})
OPTIONAL MATCH (anc:Object)-[:RELATES_TO]->(src)
  WHERE anc.unique_id <> tgt.unique_id
WITH src, tgt, e, collect(anc)[0] AS anc
OPTIONAL MATCH (tgt)-[r2:RELATES_TO]->(beyond:Object)
  WHERE beyond.unique_id <> src.unique_id
    AND r2.direction_8 = e.direction_8
WITH src, tgt, e, anc, collect(beyond)[0] AS beyond
OPTIONAL MATCH (:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[ego_r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt)
RETURN
  src.unique_id                    AS src_id,
  src.type                         AS src_type,
  coalesce(src.status, '')         AS src_status,
  tgt.unique_id                    AS tgt_id,
  tgt.type                         AS tgt_type,

  coalesce(e.predicates[1], '')    AS dist_level,
  e.distance                       AS actual_dist,

  anc.unique_id                    AS anc_id,
  anc.type                         AS anc_type,
  beyond.unique_id                 AS beyond_id,
  beyond.type                      AS beyond_type
LIMIT 1

OPTIONAL MATCH (anc:Object)-[:RELATES_TO]->(src)
  WHERE anc.unique_id <> tgt.unique_id
WITH src, tgt, e, collect(anc)[0] AS anc
OPTIONAL MATCH (tgt)-[r2:RELATES_TO]->(beyond:Object)
  WHERE beyond.unique_id <> src.unique_id
    AND r2.direction_8 = e.direction_8
WITH src, tgt, e, anc, collect(beyond)[0] AS beyond
OPTIONAL MATCH (:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[ego_r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt)
RETURN
  src.unique_id                    AS src_id,
  src.type                         AS src_type,

  anc.unique_id                    AS anc_id,
  anc.type                         AS anc_type,
  beyond.unique_id                 AS beyond_id,
  beyond.type                      AS beyond_type
LIMIT 1

**Command Executed:**
```bash
Get-Process python | Where-Object {$_.Id -eq 37176} | Stop-Process -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; Write-Output "killed"

MarkAsCompleted: 1 items
日志格式已完整。以 cell 2（`ego→car35`）为例，完整展示了你要看的每一步：

---

**Step 5a — LLM 生成 Cypher（实际调用，12.5s）**
```cypher
MATCH (src:Object {unique_id: 'ego'})-[e:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object {unique_id: 'car35'})
OPTIONAL MATCH (anc:Object)-[:RELATES_TO]->(src)
  WHERE anc.unique_id <> tgt.unique_id
WITH src, tgt, e, collect(anc)[0] AS anc
OPTIONAL MATCH (tgt)-[r2:RELATES_TO]->(beyond:Object)
  WHERE beyond.unique_id <> src.unique_id
    AND r2.direction_8 = e.direction_8
...
RETURN src.unique_id AS src_id, ..., e.direction_4 AS dir4, e.direction_8 AS dir8,
       coalesce(e.predicates[1],'') AS dist_level, e.distance AS actual_dist, ...
LIMIT 1
```
✅ 字段名全部正确（`direction_4 / direction_8 / predicates[1]`），prompt 修正生效

**Step 5b — ctx 关键字段**
```
src=ego(ego)  tgt=car35(car/stopped)
dir4=right  dir8=front-right  dist_level=far  actual_dist=29.45m
ego_dir8=front-right  anc=car1(car)  beyond=None
```
**Step 5c — 候选集 8 个**（ego 右方全部对象，含 car35 目标和 7 个干扰）

**Step 5d — 约束链逐层尝试**
```
试 type          8→5   ✗（car 有 5 辆）
试 status        8→5   ✗
试 dir8          8→6   ✗
试 type+status   8→5   ✗
试 type+dir8     8→4   ✗
试 status+dir8   8→4   ✗
试 two_hop_referent ref=car9 dir=front-left  ✅ 唯一
```
最终问题：**"What car is to the front-left of the car to the front-right of ego?"**

**Step 5d.5 — Python 自动生成验证 Cypher**（不经 LLM）
```cypher
MATCH (ref:Object {unique_id: 'car9'})-[r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object)
WHERE ref.unique_id IS NOT NULL
  AND r.direction_8 = 'front-left'
  AND tgt.type = 'car'
RETURN count(tgt) AS n, collect(tgt.unique_id) AS ids
```
验证结果：`n=1  ids=['car35']  ✅ 确认唯一`

---

**关于"验证 Cypher 谁来写"的答案**：是 `_build_verify_cypher()` 这个 Python 函数，它把约束链选出的方法（`two_hop_referent`）和属性值（`ref_id=car9, dir8=front-left, type=car`）直接翻译成 Cypher WHERE 条件。完全确定性，无 LLM，无额外网络请求。

日志命令：`--log-level DEBUG` 加入参数即可，已内置 neo4j 噪声过滤，只显示业务相关内容。

counts = collections.Counter()

        rec = json.loads(line)
        sname = rec.get('scene_name','?')
        frame = rec.get('frame_idx', 0)
        n = len([x for x in rec.get('nodes',[]) if x.get('unique_id') != 'ego'])
        counts[(sname, frame)] = n
for (s,fr), cnt in sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[:12]:

日志已经有了，完整清晰。以下是两个 cell 的完整过程，不需要重新跑：

---

## Cell 1：`ego → truck1`（1步即唯一）

```
Step 5a  LLM 生成 Cypher  13s  实际调用 ✓
Step 5b  ctx: tgt=truck1(truck/stopped)  dir4=front  dir8=front  dist=46.97m  anc=car1
Step 5c  候选集 29 个（ego 正前方所有对象）
Step 5c2 referents 10 条
约束链：
  可用属性: {type:truck, status:stopped, dir8:front}
  试 type  →  29个→1个  ✅ 唯一（场景里只有1辆卡车）

验证 Cypher（Python 生成）：
  MATCH (src:Object {unique_id:'ego'})-[r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object)
  WHERE r.direction_4='front' AND tgt.type='truck'
  RETURN count(tgt) AS n, collect(tgt.unique_id) AS ids
验证结果：n=1  ids=['truck1']  ✅ 确认唯一

最终问题："What is the truck to the front of ego?"
```
---

## Cell 2：`ego → car35`（属性全失败，最终 two_hop）

```
Step 5a  LLM 生成 Cypher  11.5s  实际调用 ✓
Step 5b  ctx: tgt=car35(car/stopped)  dir4=right  dir8=front-right  dist=29.45m  anc=car1
Step 5c  候选集 8 个（ego 右方对象）
约束链：
  可用属性: {type:car, status:stopped, dir8:front-right}
  试 type          8→5   ✗  右方有5辆 car
  试 status        8→5   ✗  stopped 也有5个
  试 dir8          8→6   ✗  front-right 有6个
  试 type+status   8→5   ✗
  试 type+dir8     8→4   ✗
  试 status+dir8   8→4   ✗
  试 two_hop ref=car9 dir=front-left  ✅ 唯一

验证 Cypher（Python 生成）：
  MATCH (ref:Object {unique_id:'car9'})-[r:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object)
  WHERE r.direction_8='front-left' AND tgt.type='car'
  RETURN count(tgt) AS n, collect(tgt.unique_id) AS ids
验证结果：n=1  ids=['car35']  ✅ 确认唯一

最终问题："What car is to the front-left of the car to the front-right of ego?"
```
---

两个典型案例，一个简单（类型即唯一），一个复杂（6次属性失败 → 跨对象参照成功），格式完整。如果想看更多或换场景，说一声就行。

USER:
来个L2级别问题的流程样例，要那种比较复杂的

AGENT:
L2 级别问题需要非 ego 的 src（比如 car1→car35），这样问题结构才能是三跳："在 ego 某方向的 A 的某方向的 B"。前面 63 个 gap cell 全是 ego→X，跳过它们找一个非 ego src 的 cell，用 DEBUG 跑完整链路：

**Command Executed:**
```bash
$py = "E:\Project\ADVTEST\.venv310\Scripts\python.exe"
& $py -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'E:/Project/ADVTEST/nuscenes_s3c_experiment/core_pipeline')
from neo4j import GraphDatabase
driver = GraphDatabase.driver('bolt://localhost:7800', auth=('neo4j','87017563'))
with driver.session() as s:
    # 找 src!=ego 且候选集不太大的 gap，展示几个候选
    r = s.run('''
        MATCH (src:Object)-[e:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object)
        WHERE src.unique_id <> 'ego'
        WITH src, tgt, e,
             size([(src)-[:RELATES_TO]->(x) | x]) AS out_degree,
             size([(y)-[:RELATES_TO]->(tgt) | y]) AS in_degree
        WHERE out_degree <= 10
        RETURN src.unique_id AS src_id, src.type AS src_type,
               tgt.unique_id AS tgt_id, tgt.type AS tgt_type,
               e.direction_4 AS dir4, e.direction_8 AS dir8,
               out_degree, in_degree
        ORDER BY out_degree ASC, in_degree DESC
        LIMIT 12
    ''')
    for rec in r:
        print(f\"{rec['src_id']:15s}({rec['src_type']:6s}) -> {rec['tgt_id']:15s}({rec['tgt_type']:6s})  dir={rec['dir8']:12s}  out={rec['out_degree']} in={rec['in_degree']}\")
driver.close()
"
```

**Output:**

$script = @'
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'E:/Project/ADVTEST/nuscenes_s3c_experiment/core_pipeline')
from neo4j import GraphDatabase
driver = GraphDatabase.driver('bolt://localhost:7800', auth=('neo4j','87017563'))
with driver.session() as s:
    r = s.run("""
        MATCH (src:Object)-[e:RELATES_TO]->(tgt:Object)
        WHERE src.unique_id <> 'ego'
        WITH src, tgt, e,
             size([(src)-[:RELATES_TO]->(x) | x]) AS out_deg
        WHERE out_deg >= 3 AND out_deg <= 8
        RETURN src.unique_id AS src_id, src.type AS src_type,
               tgt.unique_id AS tgt_id, tgt.type AS tgt_type,
               e.direction_4 AS dir4, e.direction_8 AS dir8,
               out_deg
        ORDER BY out_deg DESC
        LIMIT 10
    """)
    for rec in r:
        print(f"{rec['src_id']:15s}({rec['src_type']:6s}) -> {rec['tgt_id']:15s}({rec['tgt_type']:6s})  dir8={rec['dir8']:14s} out={rec['out_deg']}")
driver.close()
'@
